intdb {
	bel_class SLICE {
		input A[2];
		input B[2];
		input C[2];
		input D[2];
		input M[2];
		input FXA;
		input FXB;
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		output F[2];
		output Q[2];
		output OFX[2];
	}

	bel_class EBR {
		input CLKA;
		input CLKB;
		input RSTA;
		input RSTB;
		input CEA;
		input CEB;
		input WEA;
		input WEB;
		input MORCLKA;
		input MORCLKB;
		input CSA[3];
		input CSB[3];
		input ADA[13];
		input ADB[13];
		input DIA[18];
		input DIB[18];
		output DOA[18];
		output DOB[18];
	}

	bel_class IO {
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		input OPOS[2];
		input ONEG[2];
		input PADDT;
		output IPOS[2];
		output PADDI;
		output DI;
		pad PAD: inout
	}

	bel_class DQS {
		input CLK;
		input READ[1];
		output DDRCLKPOL;
		output PRMBDET;
	}

	bel_class DDRDLL {
		input CLK;
		input RST;
		input UDDCNTL;
		output LOCK;
	}

	bel_class DCC {
		input CLKI;
		input CE;
		output CLKO;
	}

	bel_class DCS {
		input CLK[2];
		input SEL[1];
		output DCSOUT;
	}

			cell CELL;

			cell IO_CLKFB;

				mux CELL.IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL.IMUX_C[0] | CELL.IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL.OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | IO_CLKFB.OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKI = CELL.IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0];
				input CLKFB = CELL.IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0];
				input RESETM = CELL.IMUX_A[1];

				input CNTRST = CELL.IMUX_D[0];
				input PWD = CELL.IMUX_C[1];
				input DDAMODE = CELL.IMUX_B[3];
				input DDAIZR = CELL.IMUX_A[2];
				input DDAILAG = CELL.IMUX_A[3];

				input DDAIDEL[1] = CELL.IMUX_C[2];
				input DDAIDEL[2] = CELL.IMUX_B[2];
				input TCLKI = CELL.IMUX_D[1];
				output CLKOP = CELL.OUT_F[5], CELL.OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0];
				output CLKOS = CELL.OUT_F[6], CELL.OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0];
				output LOCK = CELL.OUT_Q[0];
				output CLKOK = CELL.OUT_F[7], CELL.OUT_PLL_CLKOK;
				output DNLOCK = CELL.OUT_Q[3];
				output UPLOCK = CELL.OUT_Q[2];
				output DDAOZR = CELL.OUT_F[0];
				output DDAOLAG = CELL.OUT_F[4];
				output DDAODEL[0] = CELL.OUT_F[3];
				output DDAODEL[1] = CELL.OUT_F[2];
				output DDAODEL[2] = CELL.OUT_F[1];

				output CLKOS = CELL.OUT_F[6], CELL.OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0];
